## Lightweight for All-Day Comfort

[![

## Lightweight for All-Day Comfort

Designed for both style and function, the Mini Tote fits seamlessly into your daily routine. Carry it as a top handle or over the shoulder with its ergonomic straps. With its spacious interior and a dedicated inner pocket, it keeps your essentials organized and easily accessible throughout the day.

## What Fits in my Bag

## What Fits in my Bag

Designed for both style and functionality, this spacious yet elegant handbag effortlessly accommodates your daily essentials. From work must-haves to personal luxuries, it keeps everything organized and within reach, ensuring you stay stylish and prepared wherever you go.

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

Inspired by UAE’s rich heritage and modern innovation, each handbag blends minimalist design with subtle cultural accents for a look that is both timeless and contemporary. Crafted from the finest camel leather, known for its luxurious feel and natural scratch resistance, every bag is handmade with precision and care, ensuring durability that lasts for years.

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

Its clean, timeless design pairs effortlessly with everything from casual jeans to elegant abayas, making it a must-have in your wardrobe. Whether you're heading to work, meeting friends, or attending a special occasion, this bag adds a refined touch to any look while remaining practical for everyday life.

## What to Expect in Your Package

## What to Expect in Your Package

Your handbag arrives in a thoughtfully curated box, including exclusive branded stickers, a personalized note, a warranty card, and a satin dust bag to keep your bag in perfect condition.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your handbags made from?

Our handbags are crafted from premium leather or high-quality sustainable materials.

- ### Are your handbags water-resistant?

While our bags are designed to resist light splashes, they are not fully waterproof.

- ### How do I care for my handbag?

Use a damp cloth for cleaning and store it in the provided dustbag when not in use.

- ### Do your handbags come with a warranty?

Yes, all our handbags are covered by a 2-year warranty against manufacturing defects.

- ### Can I return or exchange a handbag?

Yes, we accept returns or exchanges within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original condition.

- ### Are the straps adjustable?

Yes, most of our handbags come with adjustable and removable straps for versatility.

- ### When Will My Order Ship?

It depends on where you are. Orders processed here will take 1-3 business days to arrive. Overseas deliveries can take anywhere from 3-7 days. Delivery details will be provided in your confirmation email.

## SHIPPING AND DELIVERY

Free delivery on all orders over 500 AED.

You’ll be able to select a delivery option and review prices for each option during the checkout.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Purchases made after 9 am GST will not be shipped out until the next business day. If you order after 9 am GST on a Thursday, your order will likely be shipped out on the following Saturday. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

### SHIPPING RATES & DELIVERY

| SERVICE | COST | DELIVERY TIME |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Sharjah | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: Same Day or 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| UAE - Rest of the Emirates | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\
\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

## CUSTOMER SATISFACTION GUARANTEE

**At Minimalist, your satisfaction is our highest priority. Every piece we create—whether it’s a genuine camel leather handbag, a timeless watch, or a piece of Emirati-inspired jewellery—is crafted with uncompromising quality and attention to detail.We stand behind the authenticity and craftsmanship of our products.**

- Authenticity Assured – All our products are made from genuine materials and ethically sourced.
- Premium Quality Promise – Every item undergoes strict quality checks to ensure it meets our highest standards.
- Easy Returns & Exchanges – If your purchase does not meet your expectations, you may return or exchange it within 30 days check our [return and exchange policy](/pages/new-uae-shipping "new-Us-Shipping")
- Dedicated Support – Our customer care team is always available to assist you with any concerns, ensuring a smooth and reassuring shopping experience.

SHIPPING by tomorrow

The collection is inspired by a childhood memory of love. A little girl gathered flowers for her grandmother. She then braided them into the girl's hair and drew the flower on her hand with henna. These moments are transformed into leaf-shaped jewellery, which is inspired by the petals on the flower that were collected for the grandmother, honoring her quiet strength and unconditional love. Each piece preserves a tender memory, meant to be worn close.

Leaf-Inspired Motif with Graceful Curves

Adorned in Crystals for Natural Sparkle

Lightweight Design for All-Day Wear

Hypoallergenic Materials for Sensitive Skin

Perfect for Stacking or Wearing Solo

## A memory turned into jewellery

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/9a7780c23f804641a9d979763776f922.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1769415166&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/9a7780c23f804641a9d979763776f922/9a7780c23f804641a9d979763776f922.HD-1080p-3.3Mbps-67750462.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## A memory turned into jewellery

Introducing a special collection created as a tribute to the grandmother who gave love so endlessly. Each leaf-shaped piece is crafted a radiant, polished finish, resembling the softness of her touch and memory. Pavé-set crystals are on both sides of the motif to ensure a perfect and sophisticated look

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

Every Daisy Leaf piece is a symbol of gratitude, designed to feel light, effortless, and radiant. Crafted from hypoallergenic materials and finished with luminous crystals for lasting comfort. Perfect for layering or wearing solo, this collection effortlessly blends heartfelt sentiment with everyday elegance.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Every piece comes in a premium wooden box, making it an unforgettable gift for loved ones. Each set also includes a soft suede pouch, perfect for carrying your jewelry on the go while keeping its shine intact. Enjoy exclusive stickers to add a touch of personality to your gift and celebrate unconditional love and the beauty of giving back.

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

A seamless blend of luxury and modern sophistication, this piece is designed to complement your watch effortlessly. Its refined craftsmanship and intricate details add a touch of elegance, making it a timeless accessory for both everyday wear and special occasions.

## The Process

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/7b97b4a876a64cf78f9b486cbe806fe4.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1769409149&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/7b97b4a876a64cf78f9b486cbe806fe4/7b97b4a876a64cf78f9b486cbe806fe4.HD-1080p-3.3Mbps-67744882.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## The Process

Each piece in the Daisy Collection is inspired by the memories of unconditional love, brought to life through hand-sketched designs and crafted with precision. Every item is polished and perfected, fitted with our adjustable slider clasp, creating jewellery that feels lightweight, comfortable, and effortlessly graceful for everyday wear.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are used in your jewellery ?

Our jewellery is made with high-quality materials plated with gold, silver, or rhodium, and adorned with premium crystals or stones.

- ### Will the jewellery tarnish over time?

With proper care, our jewellery is designed to last. Avoid exposure to water, perfumes, and harsh chemicals to maintain its shine.

- ### Can I clean the jewellery at home?

Yes, clean gently with a soft, dry cloth. Avoid using abrasive materials.

- ### Is your jewellery hypoallergenic?

Yes, our jewellery is nickel-free and safe for sensitive skin.

- ### Do you offer customization or engraving for jewellery ?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

- ### Does the jewellery come with a storage box or pouch?

Yes, all our jewellery comes with a complimentary velvet box or pouch for safekeeping.

- ### What is your return policy for jewellery ?

We accept returns within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original packaging.

## Lightweight for All-Day Comfort

Designed for both style and function, the Mini Tote fits seamlessly into your daily routine. Carry it as a top handle or over the shoulder with its ergonomic straps. With its spacious interior and a dedicated inner pocket, it keeps your essentials organized and easily accessible throughout the day.

## What Fits in my Bag

## What Fits in my Bag

Designed for both style and functionality, this spacious yet elegant handbag effortlessly accommodates your daily essentials. From work must-haves to personal luxuries, it keeps everything organized and within reach, ensuring you stay stylish and prepared wherever you go.

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

Inspired by UAE’s rich heritage and modern innovation, each handbag blends minimalist design with subtle cultural accents for a look that is both timeless and contemporary. Crafted from the finest camel leather, known for its luxurious feel and natural scratch resistance, every bag is handmade with precision and care, ensuring durability that lasts for years.

## What to Expect in Your Package

## What to Expect in Your Package

Your handbag arrives in a thoughtfully curated box, including exclusive branded stickers, a personalized note, a warranty card, and a satin dust bag to keep your bag in perfect condition.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- #### When Will My Order Ship?

It depends on where you are. Orders processed here will take 1-3 business days to arrive. Overseas deliveries can take anywhere from 3-7 days. Delivery details will be provided in your confirmation email.

- #### I Need To Change Something On My Order. How Can I Do That?

If you need to change or cancel your order, please contact us immediately. Once our warehouse has processed your order, we will be unable to make any changes.

- #### Is there a warranty?

MINIMALIST has a limited warranty that covers manufacturer's defects for two years after the original purchase date from an authorized dealer. Basically, if the watch parts or mechanics don't work properly, and it's our fault, we will repair or replace your product for free. However, this does not cover any abuse you might put your watch through on your own. The warranty does not cover water damage, normal wear and tear, batteries, accidental glass damage, scratches, strap damage, or theft. Proof of purchase is required for all warranty claims and service requests, so please keep your receipt. If you've purchased from our site or Amazon, the order number will suffice; otherwise if you purchased via another authorized seller or retail location, you will need to contact them directly as they handle their own warranty claims, returns and exchanges.

## Joory Flower Inspired Design with a Radiant Glow

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/0d6b46616cf2477cb799f29713265c95.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1758117419&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/0d6b46616cf2477cb799f29713265c95/0d6b46616cf2477cb799f29713265c95.HD-720p-1.6Mbps-57665050.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## Joory Flower Inspired Design with a Radiant Glow

Inspired by the delicate beauty of petite Joory roses, the Mini Joory Collection showcases soft petal forms encircling a luminous crystal center. With convertible earrings that offer two elegant ways to wear and double-sided stones that add depth from every angle, each piece captures a perfect balance of charm and versatility.

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

Designed for everyday elegance, the Mini Joory Collection is made from ultra-lightweight, hypoallergenic materials that offer all-day comfort with a luxurious feel.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Every piece comes in a premium wooden box, making it an unforgettable gift for loved ones especially for birthdays, graduation and other special occasions. Each set also includes a soft suede pouch, perfect for carrying your jewelry on the go while keeping its shine intact. Enjoy exclusive stickers to add a touch of personality to your gift, making the unboxing experience even more memorable.

## Luxurious Design that Pairs with your Watch

## Luxurious Design that Pairs with your Watch

A seamless blend of luxury and modern sophistication, this piece is designed to complement your watch effortlessly. Its refined craftsmanship and intricate details add a touch of elegance, making it a timeless accessory for both everyday wear and special occasions.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are used in your jewellery ?

Our jewellery is made with high-quality materials plated with gold, silver, or rhodium, and adorned with premium crystals or stones.

- ### Will the jewellery tarnish over time?

With proper care, our jewellery is designed to last. Avoid exposure to water, perfumes, and harsh chemicals to maintain its shine.

- ### Can I clean the jewellery at home?

Yes, clean gently with a soft, dry cloth. Avoid using abrasive materials.

- ### Is your jewellery hypoallergenic?

Yes, our jewellery is nickel-free and safe for sensitive skin.

- ### Do you offer customization or engraving for jewellery ?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

- ### Does the jewellery come with a storage box or pouch?

Yes, all our jewellery comes with a complimentary velvet box or pouch for safekeeping.

- ### What is your return policy for jewellery ?

We accept returns within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original packaging.

## Shimmering Bracelet with a Comfortable Feel

## Elegant Wooden Packaging – A Perfect Gift

## Dailywear Watch That Complements Your Outfit

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

## Lightweight for All-Day Comfort

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/d53d45501ed442949b5b5f6ea3c2e5fd.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1737791717&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/d53d45501ed442949b5b5f6ea3c2e5fd/d53d45501ed442949b5b5f6ea3c2e5fd.HD-1080p-2.5Mbps-41561904.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## Lightweight for All-Day Comfort

Crafted for all-day comfort, its long straps allow for versatile carrying—over the shoulder or as a top handle. A sleek and spacious bag designed to fit your MacBook Pro 14", iPad 13", and more. Made from scratch-resistant leather, it's the perfect blend of style and durability.

## What Fits in my Bag

## What Fits in my Bag

Designed for both style and functionality, this spacious yet elegant handbag effortlessly accommodates your daily essentials. From work must-haves to personal luxuries, it keeps everything organized and within reach, ensuring you stay stylish and prepared wherever you go.

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

## Handcrafted in UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

Inspired by UAE’s rich heritage and modern innovation, each handbag blends minimalist design with subtle cultural accents for a look that is both timeless and contemporary. Crafted from the finest camel leather, known for its luxurious feel and natural scratch resistance, every bag is handmade with precision and care, ensuring durability that lasts for years.

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

Its clean, timeless design pairs effortlessly with everything from casual jeans to elegant abayas, making it a must-have in your wardrobe. Whether you're heading to work, meeting friends, or attending a special occasion, this bag adds a refined touch to any look while remaining practical for everyday life.

## What to Expect in Your Package

## What to Expect in Your Package

Your handbag arrives in a thoughtfully curated box, including exclusive branded stickers, a personalized note, a warranty card, and a satin dust bag to keep your bag in perfect condition.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your handbags made from?

Our handbags are crafted from premium leather or high-quality sustainable materials.

- ### Are your handbags water-resistant?

While our bags are designed to resist light splashes, they are not fully waterproof.

- ### How do I care for my handbag?

Use a damp cloth for cleaning and store it in the provided dustbag when not in use.

- ### Do your handbags come with a warranty?

Yes, all our handbags are covered by a 2-year warranty against manufacturing defects.

- ### Can I return or exchange a handbag?

Yes, we accept returns or exchanges within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original condition.

- ### Are the straps adjustable?

Yes, most of our handbags come with adjustable and removable straps for versatility.

- ### When Will My Order Ship?

It depends on where you are. Orders processed here will take 1-3 business days to arrive. Overseas deliveries can take anywhere from 3-7 days. Delivery details will be provided in your confirmation email.

# Care and Maintenance

## LEATHER CARE

Use a soft dry cloth to remove any dust or dirt

Avoid using wipes containing alcohol, fragrance, or dye.

Keep away from water and perfumes

Keep in a cool and dry place, away from direct sunlight

Cover the bag in a dust bag to avoid scratches

Avoid placing sharp objects such as keys and jewelry

Leather stretches, so avoid overfilling to maintain its shape

[SHOP LEATHER GOODS](/collections/all-leather-goods)

## Jewellery Care

Store Jewellery In A Soft Lined Box. Do Not Store Jewellery Over-Lapped.

Please Remove Jewellery Before Washing Hands. Avoid Contact With Water.

PROTECT YOUR JEWELLERY FROM SHARP BOWS AND SCRATCHES

AVOID DIRECT CONTACT WITH PERFUME, BODY LOTIONS OR OTHER CHEMICALS.

ALL STONES ARE HAND SET INTO THE JEWELLERY. EACH PIECE IS UNIQUE AND MAY VARY IN COLOUR.

AVOID WEARING WHILE CLEANING OR PERFORMING SPORTS ACTIVITIES

## Jewellery Care

Store Jewellery In A Soft Lined Box. Do Not Store Jewellery Over-Lapped.

Please Remove Jewellery Before Washing Hands. Avoid Contact With Water.

PROTECT YOUR JEWELLERY FROM SHARP BOWS AND SCRATCHES

AVOID DIRECT CONTACT WITH PERFUME, BODY LOTIONS OR OTHER CHEMICALS.

ALL STONES ARE HAND SET INTO THE JEWELLERY. EACH PIECE IS UNIQUE AND MAY VARY IN COLOUR.

AVOID WEARING WHILE CLEANING OR PERFORMING SPORTS ACTIVITIES

[SHOP JEWELLERY](/collections/jewellery)

[SHOP JEWELLERY](/collections/jewellery)

## WATCH CARE

WIPE THE WATCH WITH A SOFT, LINT-FREE CLOTH TO REMOVE DUST, FINGERPRINTS, AND MOISTURE

CLEAN METAL BRACELETS REGULARLY TO PREVENT THE BUILDUP OF DIRT AND OILS., USE A DRY SOFT BRUSH TO CLEAN THE LINKS

TRY TO KEEP YOU WATCH AWAY FROM MAGNETS

DO NOT OPEN TO REPAIR YOUR WATCH ALL BY YOURSELF

Store your watch in the watch box when not in use to avoid scratches

Avoid contact with chemicals such as cleaning supplies and perfumes

## WATCH CARE

WIPE THE WATCH WITH A SOFT, LINT-FREE CLOTH TO REMOVE DUST, FINGERPRINTS, AND MOISTURE

CLEAN METAL BRACELETS REGULARLY TO PREVENT THE BUILDUP OF DIRT AND OILS., USE A DRY SOFT BRUSH TO CLEAN THE LINKS

TRY TO KEEP YOU WATCH AWAY FROM MAGNETS

DO NOT OPEN TO REPAIR YOUR WATCH ALL BY YOURSELF

Store your watch in the watch box when not in use to avoid scratches

Avoid contact with chemicals such as cleaning supplies and perfumes

[SHOP WATCHES](/collections/mens-watches)

[SHOP WATCHES](/collections/mens-watches)

## SUNGLASSES CARE

Gently cleanse your eyewear with soap and water.

Keep your eyewear in a case and store it in a dry place with a temperature range on 10 degree C to 35 degree C

Avoid exposure to direct sunlight, artificial UV radiation and any shocks that could break or damage your eyewear.

Be careful to never place the lenses face down

Never use solvents, alcohol or abrasive cloths

## SHIPPING AND DELIVERY

Free delivery on all orders over 500 AED.

You’ll be able to select a delivery option and review prices for each option during the checkout.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Purchases made after 9 am GST will not be shipped out until the next business day. If you order after 9 am GST on a Thursday, your order will likely be shipped out on the following Saturday. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

### SHIPPING RATES & DELIVERY

| SERVICE | COST | DELIVERY TIME |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Sharjah | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: Same Day or 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| UAE - Rest of the Emirates | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\
\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

## CUSTOMER SATISFACTION GUARANTEE

**At Minimalist, your satisfaction is our highest priority. Every piece we create—whether it’s a genuine camel leather handbag, a timeless watch, or a piece of Emirati-inspired jewellery—is crafted with uncompromising quality and attention to detail.We stand behind the authenticity and craftsmanship of our products.**

- Authenticity Assured – All our products are made from genuine materials and ethically sourced.
- Premium Quality Promise – Every item undergoes strict quality checks to ensure it meets our highest standards.
- Easy Returns & Exchanges – If your purchase does not meet your expectations, you may return or exchange it within 30 days check our [return and exchange policy](/pages/new-uae-shipping "new-Us-Shipping")
- Dedicated Support – Our customer care team is always available to assist you with any concerns, ensuring a smooth and reassuring shopping experience.

| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\
\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

## CUSTOMER SATISFACTION GUARANTEE

**At Minimalist, your satisfaction is our highest priority. Every piece we create—whether it’s a genuine camel leather handbag, a timeless watch, or a piece of Emirati-inspired jewellery—is crafted with uncompromising quality and attention to detail.We stand behind the authenticity and craftsmanship of our products.**

- Authenticity Assured – All our products are made from genuine materials and ethically sourced.
- Premium Quality Promise – Every item undergoes strict quality checks to ensure it meets our highest standards.
- Easy Returns & Exchanges – If your purchase does not meet your expectations, you may return or exchange it within 30 days check our [return and exchange policy](/pages/new-uae-shipping "new-Us-Shipping")
- Dedicated Support – Our customer care team is always available to assist you with any concerns, ensuring a smooth and reassuring shopping experience.

## Brilliant Sparkle with Lasting Comfort

The brilliant, high-quality crystals on the dial are designed to maintain their radiant sparkle, catching the light with every movement. Crafted with ergonomic precision, the watch follows the wrist’s natural curve, ensuring a comfortable, secure fit that feels comfortable all day long.

## Luxurious and Timeless Addition to Your Jewellery Collection

## Luxurious and Timeless Addition to Your Jewellery Collection

Designed to complement your finest jewellery, the watch’s unique profile and shimmering accents enhance every outfit. Whether worn alone or layered with bracelets, its classic yet modern appeal makes it the perfect finishing touch for any occasion.

## Lightweight Mesh Strap for Effortless Fit

## Lightweight Mesh Strap for Effortless Fit

The smooth mesh strap adapts to your wrist for a comfortable, tailored fit. With a lightweight feel and elegant finish, it transitions seamlessly from day to night, adding effortless sophistication to every look.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Elegantly packaged in a polished wooden box, this watch is a perfect gift for loved ones, ideal for weddings, anniversaries, and special milestones. The refined packaging enhances the gifting experience, while a set of exclusive stickers adds a personal touch, making every unboxing moment truly special.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

#### MINIMALIST WATCHES WITH HIGH-FASHION APPEAL

Give new meaning to quality time by [gifting](/collections/gift-set "Gift Set") yourself or a loved-one a gift-set of our designer watches for women. With thoughtfully crafted **Minimalist** designs, we strike the perfect balance between being chic and professional. Our wristwatches make the perfect final touch to any outfit, from weddings to corporate events to a girls' night out party.

#### OWN YOUR TIME WITH EXQUISITE LADIES WATCHES FROM OUR COLLECTION

Effortlessly being yourself is the best you can be. At **Minimalist**, we design women's Luxury watches that empower you to be the star of your own show. For modern women, a watch is one of the most important part of accessories that you own. Not only does it help you manage your time, but it plays a massive significant role creating the first impression you make throughout your day. At the same time, we don't compromise on comfort, providing you with the freedom to just be you.

#### BE UNIQUE IN EVERY ASPECT.

As modern women, we understand that you play multiple roles hence we offer a catalogue of designer watches to support you. From bold and assertive to delicate and soothing designs, you will find our watches branded for women that suit any occasion. Our collection spans a wide variety of hues, styles, dial shapes, and band designs so you can showcase your one-in-a-million / unique personality.

#### GIFT YOURSELF A GORGEOUS AND LUXURIOUS TIME

Thanks to our ability to satisfy our customers' demands and their continued faith in us, Minimalist is one of the top branded watches for retailers in the UAE. We are happy to announce that we reward our loyal customers with free shipping and fast delivery. Our products also come with a two-year warranty so you can enjoy peace of mind. We thrive to provide you with the best of service. However, if you feel we fell short in any way, we will be happy to offer you a refund or exchange of your preference.

## SHIPPING AND DELIVERY

Free delivery on all orders over 500 AED.

You’ll be able to select a delivery option and review prices for each option during the checkout.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Purchases made after 9 am GST will not be shipped out until the next business day. If you order after 9 am GST on a Thursday, your order will likely be shipped out on the following Saturday. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

### SHIPPING RATES & DELIVERY

| SERVICE | COST | DELIVERY TIME |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Sharjah | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: Same Day or 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| UAE - Rest of the Emirates | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\
\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

What Fits in my Bag

## Lightweight Elegance, Rooted in Tradition

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/7c5e12c633b444df89ab7482773b2052.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1755517269&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/7c5e12c633b444df89ab7482773b2052/7c5e12c633b444df89ab7482773b2052.HD-720p-1.6Mbps-55095632.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## Lightweight Elegance, Rooted in Tradition

Designed to honour the strength and grace of Emirati women, the Maitha handbag combines a compact, lightweight form with timeless detail. Its vanity-inspired silhouette and double zip closure offer everyday practicality, while soft structure and refined finishes reflect the quiet elegance that defines modern femininity. Handcrafted in the UAE, it’s a thoughtful balance of purpose, tradition, and style.

## What Fits in my Bag

## What Fits in my Bag

Thoughtfully sized to carry your daily essentials, the Maitha handbag holds everything you need without the bulk. From your phone and cardholder to makeup and keys, it keeps everything neatly organised and easy to access. Perfect for both day plans and evening outings.

## Handcrafted in the UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

## Handcrafted in the UAE with the Finest Camel Leather

Inspired by UAE’s rich heritage and modern innovation, each handbag blends minimalist design with subtle cultural accents for a look that is both timeless and contemporary. Crafted from the finest camel leather, known for its luxurious feel and natural scratch resistance, every bag is handmade with precision and care, ensuring durability that lasts for years.

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

## Versatile and Stylish to Match Any Outfit

Its clean, timeless design pairs effortlessly with everything from casual jeans to elegant abayas, making it a must-have in your wardrobe. Whether you're heading to work, meeting friends, or attending a special occasion, this bag adds a refined touch to any look while remaining practical for everyday life.

## What to Expect in Your Package

## What to Expect in Your Package

Your handbag arrives in a thoughtfully curated box, including exclusive branded stickers, a personalized note, a warranty card, and a satin dust bag to keep your bag in perfect condition.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your handbags made from?

Our handbags are crafted from premium leather or high-quality sustainable materials.

- ### Are your handbags water-resistant?

While our bags are designed to resist light splashes, they are not fully waterproof.

- ### How do I care for my handbag?

Use a damp cloth for cleaning and store it in the provided dustbag when not in use.

- ### Do your handbags come with a warranty?

Yes, all our handbags are covered by a 2-year warranty against manufacturing defects.

- ### Can I return or exchange a handbag?

Yes, we accept returns or exchanges within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original condition.

- ### Are the straps adjustable?

Yes, most of our handbags come with adjustable and removable straps for versatility.

- ### When Will My Order Ship?

It depends on where you are. Orders processed here will take 1-3 business days to arrive. Overseas deliveries can take anywhere from 3-7 days. Delivery details will be provided in your confirmation email.

## Engineered for Style & Performance

Inspired by vintage racing watches, this collection blends classic design with modern engineering. Powered by a Seiko mecha-quartz movement, it offers chronograph stopwatch functionality with the precision of quartz and the feel of a mechanical timepiece. Designed to be lightweight, comfortable, and perfectly contoured to the wrist for all-day wear.

## Interchangeable Steel & Leather Straps for Versatility

## Interchangeable Steel & Leather Straps for Versatility

Switch between the sleek stainless steel bracelet for a polished, refined look or the genuine leather strap for a more classic, relaxed feel. Designed for both comfort and flexibility, each strap offers a secure fit and adapts effortlessly to your style whether you're dressing up or keeping it casual.

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

Presented in a meticulously crafted wooden box, the Vintage Chrono Collection makes a memorable gift for those who appreciate style and craftsmanship. The premium packaging elevates the unboxing experience, complete with a warranty card, microfiber cloth, and fun stickers that add a thoughtful and personal touch.

## Essential Timepiece for Every Collection

## Essential Timepiece for Every Collection

Blending vintage-inspired design with modern functionality, this watch is ideal for work, travel, or everyday wear. The Arabic numerals add a refined cultural touch, making it a standout piece that complements your style and fits seamlessly into any collection.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

## SHIPPING AND DELIVERY

Free delivery on all orders over 500 AED.

You’ll be able to select a delivery option and review prices for each option during the checkout.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Purchases made after 9 am GST will not be shipped out until the next business day. If you order after 9 am GST on a Thursday, your order will likely be shipped out on the following Saturday. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

### SHIPPING RATES & DELIVERY

| SERVICE | COST | DELIVERY TIME |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Sharjah | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: Same Day or 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| UAE - Rest of the Emirates | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

[![

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

Sleek, modern, and effortlessly elegant, the fyne collection features elongated oval shapes adorned with marquise-cut crystals . Lightweight and versatile, each piece is designed for daily wear — whether you're heading to work, meeting friends, or dressing up for a special occasion. Timeless sparkle with a contemporary edge.

## Adjustable Size with Two-Sided Stones

## Adjustable Size with Two-Sided Stones

Effortless style meets versatility with reversible two-sided stones, allowing for easy styling to match any occasion. Designed with an adjustable fit, it ensures a perfect, comfortable wear—making it a thoughtful and hassle-free gift for your loved ones.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Every piece comes in a premium wooden box, making it an unforgettable gift for loved ones especially for birthdays, graduation and other special occasions. Each set also includes a soft suede pouch, perfect for carrying your jewelry on the go while keeping its shine intact. Enjoy exclusive stickers to add a touch of personality to your gift, making the unboxing experience even more memorable.

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

A seamless blend of luxury and modern sophistication, this piece is designed to complement your watch effortlessly. Its refined craftsmanship and intricate details add a touch of elegance, making it a timeless accessory for both everyday wear and special occasions.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are used in your jewellery ?

Our jewellery is made with high-quality materials plated with gold, silver, or rhodium, and adorned with premium crystals or stones.

- ### Will the jewellery tarnish over time?

With proper care, our jewellery is designed to last. Avoid exposure to water, perfumes, and harsh chemicals to maintain its shine.

- ### Can I clean the jewellery at home?

Yes, clean gently with a soft, dry cloth. Avoid using abrasive materials.

- ### Is your jewellery hypoallergenic?

Yes, our jewellery is nickel-free and safe for sensitive skin.

- ### Do you offer customization or engraving for jewellery ?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

- ### Does the jewellery come with a storage box or pouch?

Yes, all our jewellery comes with a complimentary velvet box or pouch for safekeeping.

- ### What is your return policy for jewellery ?

We accept returns within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original packaging.

## Engineered for Style & Performance

## Engineered for Style & Performance

Inspired by vintage racing watches, this collection blends classic design with modern engineering. Powered by a Seiko mecha-quartz movement, it offers chronograph stopwatch functionality with the precision of quartz and the feel of a mechanical timepiece. Designed to be lightweight, comfortable, and perfectly contoured to the wrist for all-day wear.

## Interchangeable Steel & Leather Straps for Versatility

## Interchangeable Steel & Leather Straps for Versatility

Switch between the sleek stainless steel bracelet for a polished, refined look or the genuine leather strap for a more classic, relaxed feel. Designed for both comfort and flexibility, each strap offers a secure fit and adapts effortlessly to your style whether you're dressing up or keeping it casual.

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

Presented in a meticulously crafted wooden box, the Vintage Chrono Collection makes a memorable gift for those who appreciate style and craftsmanship. The premium packaging elevates the unboxing experience, complete with a warranty card, microfiber cloth, and fun stickers that add a thoughtful and personal touch.

## Essential Timepiece for Every Collection

## Essential Timepiece for Every Collection

Blending vintage-inspired design with modern functionality, this watch is ideal for work, travel, or everyday wear. The Arabic numerals add a refined cultural touch, making it a standout piece that complements your style and fits seamlessly into any collection.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

## Open Skeleton Design with Bold Aesthetics

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/0b1e685405eb4daf94a00a9eb0c0e0e7.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1752323327&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/0b1e685405eb4daf94a00a9eb0c0e0e7/0b1e685405eb4daf94a00a9eb0c0e0e7.HD-1080p-2.5Mbps-51106334.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## Open Skeleton Design with Bold Aesthetics

The open-faced skeleton design showcases the intricate mechanics of the watch, offering a bold, modern aesthetic that sets it apart. Designed for all-day wear, its bold build and ergonomic fit ensure comfort while naturally following the movement of your wrist for a seamless experience.

## Interchangeable Straps for Versatility

## Interchangeable Straps for Versatility

Effortlessly switch between the sleek stainless steel bracelet for a polished, refined look or the durable rubber strap for a sporty, casual feel. The quick-release mechanism allows for easy strap changes, ensuring you can adapt your style to any occasion with speed and convenience. Designed for comfort and flexibility, both options provide a secure fit for all-day wear.

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

## Premium Packaging for a Timeless Gift

Presented in a meticulously crafted wooden box, the Skeleton Collection makes an unforgettable gift for watch enthusiasts. The premium packaging enhances the unboxing experience, while the included warranty card, microfiber cloth, and fun stickers add a personal touch to every purchase.

## Timeless Heritage with Arabic Numerals

## Timeless Heritage with Arabic Numerals

The use of Arabic numerals on the dial is a nod to cultural heritage and pride, adding a meaningful touch to its contemporary design. It celebrates tradition while embracing modern craftsmanship, making it a unique addition to any collection.

## Luminous Hands and Numbers for Enhanced Visibility

## Luminous Hands and Numbers for Enhanced Visibility

The luminous hands and Arabic numerals provide superior readability in low-light conditions, making this watch as practical as it is visually striking. Whether you're traveling at night or navigating busy environments, its enhanced visibility ensures you can always keep track of time.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

## SHIPPING AND DELIVERY

Free delivery on all orders over 500 AED.

You’ll be able to select a delivery option and review prices for each option during the checkout.

After your payment is verified, it may take up to 24 hours to process and ship your order. This does not include weekends or holidays. Purchases made after 9 am GST will not be shipped out until the next business day. If you order after 9 am GST on a Thursday, your order will likely be shipped out on the following Saturday. Please refer to the below information for rates/times.

### SHIPPING RATES & DELIVERY

| SERVICE | COST | DELIVERY TIME |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dubai, Abu Dhabi, Sharjah | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: Same Day or 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| UAE - Rest of the Emirates | FREE for orders over 500 AED   20 AED for orders below 500 AED  25 AED for all COD orders | ARAMEX DELIVERY TIME: 1-3 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Over order value of 500 AED) | FREE | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |
| INTERNATIONAL ORDERS (Below order value of 500 AED) | 40 AED | DHL DELIVERY TIME: 2-4 BUSINESS DAYS |

If you're not happy for any reason, you can return any of your purchased MINIMALIST items within 30 days of receiving your order, provided the items are unused and in their original condition. You may initiate your return by contacting us on [+971 58 567 5555](tel:+971585675555).\

## 24 Months warranty

Minimalist offers a two-year international warranty from the date of purchase, covering manufacturer defects.

To make a warranty claim, a valid proof of purchase(order number for online purchases or an invoice for in-store purchases) is required.

For items bought from an authorized retailer, please contact them directly, as they manage their own warranty claims, returns, and exchanges.

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/8d8a86d0e18b440dbe9bbc8bd2469127.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1747987255&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/8d8a86d0e18b440dbe9bbc8bd2469127/8d8a86d0e18b440dbe9bbc8bd2469127.HD-720p-1.6Mbps-48095520.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## Lightweight & Comfortable for Daily Wear

Sleek, modern, and effortlessly elegant, the fyne collection features elongated oval shapes adorned with marquise-cut crystals . Lightweight and versatile, each piece is designed for daily wear — whether you're heading to work, meeting friends, or dressing up for a special occasion. Timeless sparkle with a contemporary edge.

## Adjustable Size with Two-Sided Stones

## Adjustable Size with Two-Sided Stones

Effortless style meets versatility with reversible two-sided stones, allowing for easy styling to match any occasion. Designed with an adjustable fit, it ensures a perfect, comfortable wear—making it a thoughtful and hassle-free gift for your loved ones.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Every piece comes in a premium wooden box, making it an unforgettable gift for loved ones especially for birthdays, graduation and other special occasions. Each set also includes a soft suede pouch, perfect for carrying your jewelry on the go while keeping its shine intact. Enjoy exclusive stickers to add a touch of personality to your gift, making the unboxing experience even more memorable.

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

## Luxurious design that pairs with your watch

A seamless blend of luxury and modern sophistication, this piece is designed to complement your watch effortlessly. Its refined craftsmanship and intricate details add a touch of elegance, making it a timeless accessory for both everyday wear and special occasions.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are used in your jewellery ?

Our jewellery is made with high-quality materials plated with gold, silver, or rhodium, and adorned with premium crystals or stones.

- ### Will the jewellery tarnish over time?

With proper care, our jewellery is designed to last. Avoid exposure to water, perfumes, and harsh chemicals to maintain its shine.

- ### Can I clean the jewellery at home?

Yes, clean gently with a soft, dry cloth. Avoid using abrasive materials.

- ### Is your jewellery hypoallergenic?

Yes, our jewellery is nickel-free and safe for sensitive skin.

- ### Do you offer customization or engraving for jewellery ?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

- ### Does the jewellery come with a storage box or pouch?

Yes, all our jewellery comes with a complimentary velvet box or pouch for safekeeping.

- ### What is your return policy for jewellery ?

We accept returns within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original packaging.

## Effortless Glamour with Swiss Precision

## Signature Wooden Packaging – A Timeless Gift

## A Versatile Statement That Pairs Effortlessly with Jewellery

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

## A Luxurious Eid Fragrance

[![
](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/files/preview_images/88dc26284b7743d9ac6d92d532c134ec.thumbnail.0000000000.jpg?v=1742387309&width=800)](//minimalist.ae/cdn/shop/videos/c/vp/88dc26284b7743d9ac6d92d532c134ec/88dc26284b7743d9ac6d92d532c134ec.HD-1080p-2.5Mbps-44447091.mp4?v=0#t=0.001)

## A Luxurious Eid Fragrance

A fragrance of depth and distinction, crafted exclusively for Eid. Saffron Musk is a luxurious blend of rich saffron, warm amber, and velvety musk. It opens with a fresh burst of bergamot, adding an unexpected brightness before settling into a deep, musky base. A modern yet timeless scent, perfect for gifting this Eid.

## Perfume Notes

## Perfume Notes

Saffron Musk opens with the rich, golden warmth of saffron, infused with the vibrant zest of bergamot for a bright, citrusy touch. At its heart, warm amber and earthy patchouli envelop the fragrance in deep, comforting warmth. Finally, velvety musk and refined vetiver leave a smooth, long-lasting trail.

## Art In A Bottle

## Art In A Bottle

Saffron Musk is encased in a frosted amber bottle, elegantly contrasted by golden details on the regal crown and label. The warm amber hue reflects the richness of saffron and the festive spirit of Eid, seamlessly blending with the bottle’s contemporary arabesque heritage design.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### Are your perfumes long-lasting?

Yes, our perfumes are made with high-quality ingredients for a long-lasting fragrance.

- ### What size bottles do you offer?

We offer 100 ml size and a pocket-sized options for on-the-go use.

- ### Are your perfumes alcohol-free?

Some of our perfumes are alcohol-free. Please check the product details for specific information.

- ### Are your perfumes inspired by cultural heritage?

Yes, our perfumes are crafted with traditional Arabic ingredients like oud, saffron, and rose.

- ### How should I store my perfume?

Store your perfume in a cool, dry place away from direct sunlight to preserve its quality.

- ### Do you offer gift wrapping for perfumes?

Yes, all perfumes come in luxurious packaging with an option for free gift wrapping.

Contact Us

## FREQUENTLY ASKED QUESTIONS

### Shipping & Payment Information

- #### When Will My Order Ship?

It depends on where you are. Orders processed here will take 1-3 business days to arrive. Overseas deliveries can take anywhere from 3-7 days. Delivery details will be provided in your confirmation email.

- #### Will I Have To Pay International Taxes & Duties?

Your order may be subject to import duties and taxes, which are levied once a shipment reaches your country. The general amount for the duties and taxes fee is about 20% of the dollar amount of the merchandise. However, this is just a general guideline and may vary depending on the country to which the order was shipped. You should contact your customs office for specific amounts and percentages.

MINIMALIST cannot control and is not responsible for any duties/taxes applied to your package. You will be responsible for paying additional charges for customs clearance. Customs policies vary widely from country to country; please contact your local customs office for further information. Note, in rare occasions custom agents may delay delivery of some packages.

- #### How are taxes and duties calculated?

Duties Delivered Unpaid (DDU) Orders: As the customer, you are liable for all import duties, customs and taxes levied. Payment of these is required to release your order from customs on arrival.

- #### What Payment methods you accept ?

We accept 2 Payment methods for now;\
1- E-Payment (Debit & Credit card)\
2- COD (Cash on delivery for UAE & KSA Only) \
\

- #### Why am I paying extra Charges?

Extra Charges can be for; \
1- Custom fees \
2- Currency conversion fees

- #### How can I track my order?

You can track your order via this link\
[https://track.minimalist.ae/](https://track.minimalist.ae/ "https://track.minimalist.ae/")

- #### How can I contact the courier company of my order?

Aramex KW:+9651820011\
Aramex KSA: +966920027447\
Aramex Bahrain: +97317330434\
Aramex Oman: +96824473000\
\
DHL KW: +9651808345\
DHL KSA: +966920003450 / +966138829193 / 800 3450000 / +966-12-669 3399 / +966 13 8317000 / +966 13 882 7777 / +966-11-462 1919\
DHL Oman: +96824520100 / 80077008\
DHL Bahrain: +973 1736 4100\
DHL Qatar: +97444587888

- #### What should i do if the payment is on hold?

Contact your bank.

### Order Information & Concerns

- #### I Need To Change Something On My Order. How Can I Do That?

If you need to change or cancel your order, please [contact us](mailto:support@minimalist.ae "mailto:support@minimalist.ae") immediately. Once our warehouse has processed your order, we will be unable to make any changes.

- #### My Order Status Says "Unfulfilled". What Does That Mean?

"Unfulfilled" just means that we successfully received your order! Once your order is shipped, you'll receive a tracking number and your order status will change to "Fulfilled."

- #### Why Was My Order Canceled?

If your order was unexpectedly cancelled, chances are that our fraud filter marked your order as fraudulent. If you are certain that that is not true, please order again with a Paypal account. If you do not have Paypal, you can easily sign up at www.Paypal.com

- #### Can I change my address?

Yes we can change the address if the item was not Fulfilled but in case we handover the order to a courier company you may contact them to correct the address.

- #### Can I add or remove items from the order that I just placed?

Contact the [**Customer support**](https://www.minimalist.ae/pages/contact_us "Customer support") to remove & add items.

### WATCHES

- #### What type of movement is used?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- #### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- #### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- #### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- #### Why are the hands on my chrono subdials not pointing straight up?

This is actually a very simple fix, please see the instructions below.

**If the far left sub-dial is not centered, you will need to press the correct button (reference above) continuously. The stopwatch minute hand (far left dial) is synchronized with stopwatch second hand (middle dial).**

Hope this makes sense, if you have ANY issues please don't hesitate to ask.

- #### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- #### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please [contact us](/pages/new_contact "new_contact") for further details.

- #### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- #### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- #### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- #### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

### HANDBAGS

- #### What materials are your handbags made from?

Our handbags are crafted from premium leather or high-quality sustainable materials.

- #### Are your handbags water-resistant?

While our bags are designed to resist light splashes, they are not fully waterproof.

- #### How do I care for my handbag?

Use a damp cloth for cleaning and store it in the provided dustbag when not in use.

- #### Do your handbags come with a warranty?

Yes, all our handbags are covered by a 2-year warranty against manufacturing defects.

- #### Can I return or exchange a handbag?

Yes, we accept returns or exchanges within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original condition.

- #### Are the straps adjustable?

Yes, most of our handbags come with adjustable and removable straps for versatility.

### JEWELLERY

- #### What materials are used in your jewelry?

Our jewelry is made with high-quality materials plated with gold, silver, or rhodium, and adorned with premium crystals or stones.

- #### Is your jewelry hypoallergenic?

Yes, our jewelry is nickel-free and safe for sensitive skin.

- #### Will the jewelry tarnish over time?

With proper care, our jewelry is designed to last. Avoid exposure to water, perfumes, and harsh chemicals to maintain its shine.

- #### Can I clean the jewelry at home?

Yes, clean gently with a soft, dry cloth. Avoid using abrasive materials.

- #### Do you offer customization or engraving for jewelry?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

- #### Does the jewelry come with a storage box or pouch?

Yes, all our jewelry comes with a complimentary velvet box or pouch for safekeeping.

- #### What is your return policy for jewelry?

We accept returns within 30 days of purchase if the item is unused and in its original packaging.

### WALLETS

- #### What materials are your wallets made from?

Our wallets are made from premium camel leather

- #### Do the wallets have enough card slots?

Our wallets are designed with ample card slots and compartments to suit your needs. Check the product description for exact details.

- #### Can I clean the wallet at home?

Yes, wipe gently with a damp cloth and let it air dry. Avoid using harsh chemicals.

- #### Do the wallets come with a warranty?

Yes, our wallets are backed by a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- #### Can I gift wrap the wallet?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for wallets.

### PERFUMES

- #### Are your perfumes long-lasting?

Yes, our perfumes are made with high-quality ingredients for a long-lasting fragrance.

### Other Information

- #### What is your return/exchange policy?

Please refer to our [**Returns**](/pages/return "Return") page

- #### Do You Have Any Retail Locations?

Yes, we have 2 stores across UAE  - [**MIRDIFF CITY CENTER**, **DUBAI**](https://www.google.com/maps/place/Minimalist+-+Mirdif+City+Centre/@25.2143412,55.4048532,17z/data=!3m1!4b1!4m6!3m5!1s0x3e5f61c19af669e3:0x7e04a15c96e06ad9!8m2!3d25.2143412!4d55.4074281!16s%2Fg%2F11ks12gxyj?entry=ttu - #### How do I become a MINIMALIST reseller? Do you have any authorized resellers?

While we appreciate that you want to be a reseller of MINIMALIST, we do not currently have any reseller or wholesale program. Please email us at [support@minimalist.ae](mailto:support@minimalist.ae "mailto:support@minimalist.ae") and we may get back to you when we decide to go down that path!

- #### What is the time frame for Refund, Exchange, Repair and Replacement?

- Refund - 1 to 2 W/D for Epayment / For COD we Ask the customers for a refund or store credit and it takes 2 to 3 W/D \
- Exchange - 4 to 6 Days\
- Replacement - 4 to 6 Days \
- Repair - 7 to 14 Days

- #### Minimalist store Credit and how to use it ?

Contact the [**Customer support**](https://www.minimalist.ae/pages/contact_us "Customer Support") to guide you in how to use the store credit.

- #### DO I HAVE TO PAY A CUSTOM FEE IF I RETURN AN ITEM

The customers need to pay for the custom fees whenever they return an item.\
\

## Brilliant Sparkle with Lasting Comfort

The brilliant, high-quality crystals on the dial are designed to maintain their radiant sparkle, catching the light with every movement. Crafted with ergonomic precision, the watch follows the wrist’s natural curve, ensuring a comfortable, secure fit that feels comfortable all day long.

## Luxurious and Timeless Addition to Your Jewellery Collection

## Luxurious and Timeless Addition to Your Jewellery Collection

Designed to complement your finest jewellery, the watch’s unique profile and shimmering accents enhance every outfit. Whether worn alone or layered with bracelets, its classic yet modern appeal makes it the perfect finishing touch for any occasion.

## Lightweight Mesh Strap for Effortless Fit

## Lightweight Mesh Strap for Effortless Fit

The smooth mesh strap adapts to your wrist for a comfortable, tailored fit. With a lightweight feel and elegant finish, it transitions seamlessly from day to night, adding effortless sophistication to every look.

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

## Signature Wooden Packaging, Perfect for Gifting

Elegantly packaged in a polished wooden box, this watch is a perfect gift for loved ones, ideal for weddings, anniversaries, and special milestones. The refined packaging enhances the gifting experience, while a set of exclusive stickers adds a personal touch, making every unboxing moment truly special.

## Questions? We Have Answers.

- ### What materials are your watches made from?

Our watches are crafted from premium stainless steel with sapphire crystal glass for scratch resistance and durability.

- ### How do I replace my battery?

All of our watch batteries can be found at any electronic store, as well as most watch repair or jewelry shops. Any watch repair shop or Jeweler should be able to assist you with the battery replacement. We don't suggest replacing the battery, as it requires some special tools and you could damage the watch.

- ### What type of battery is used?

Our watches use either SONY SR626SW or SONY SR621SW. Please contact us for further details.

- ### How do I maintain my watch?

Clean your watch gently with a soft cloth and avoid exposing it to extreme temperatures or strong chemicals.

- ### Are your watches water-resistant?

Yes, our watches are water-resistant, designed to withstand everyday splashes and light rain. Please check the product details for specific water resistance levels.

- ### What type of movement do your watches use?

Our watches feature reliable quartz or hybrid chronograph movements for accurate timekeeping.

- ### Do your watches come with a warranty?

Yes, all our watches come with a 2-year warranty covering manufacturing defects.

- ### Are the straps adjustable or replaceable?

Yes, most of our watch straps are adjustable and replaceable. Please refer to the product description for compatibility.

- ### Do you offer free gift wrapping?

Yes, we offer complimentary gift wrapping for all our watches.

- ### Do you offer watch customization or engraving?

Currently, we do not offer customization, but stay tuned for updates on this feature.

#### SHOW YOU CARE - GIFT THAT SPECIAL PERSON IN YOUR LIFE WITH A TOKEN OF ADMIRATION AND APPRECIATION

Finding a perfect gift for that special person in your life can be a challenge. Luckily, we've got the ideal solution: a gift watch set. A watch is an evergreen gift that's perfect for every occasion. Whether it's for a graduation, job promotion, or a mother's/father's day gift, it's the ideal way to make someone feel appreciated. On top of inspiring joy and pride, it's a constant reminder of your connection that they will carry closely with them.

At [Minimalist](https://www.minimalist.ae/), we offer a wide variety of gift sets delivered in an attractive and immaculate gift box.

#### A CONSTANT REMINDER OF YOUR AFFECTION

As a gift, a watch symbolizes your deep care and affection for that special person in your life. Worn on the wrist, it's a daily reminder of the person that gifted it. Every time the wearer looks down to know the time, they'll be reminded of you. As a parting gift, it can help you feel closer together over long distances.

#### SELECT THE PERFECT GIFT FROM OUR COLLECTION

No matter who the person is or their sense of style, you'll find thate perfect gift among our collection of watch sets. For gifting watches to men, we offer a wide range of professional, modern and rugged style for adventurous lifestyles. When it comes to women's gift sets, you'll find various combinations of styles with matching jewellery. We also offer his/her sets to gift couples for engagement, wedding, or on the birth of their a child.

You can easily choose from the popular range of gift [watches available on Minimalist](/ "/"), such as Sheikha/Joory, Hayaa, Amber, Roudha, and the list goes on.

Our gift watch sets come at a budget-friendly price with 24 months warranty as well as easy returns, refunds, and exchanges. We also offer a free and fast delivery service to not keep your special person waiting.
